/*
 * Lets Node's built-in test runner load the app's TypeScript modules
 * (Node 24 strips types natively). Module resolution is customised:
 * - "@/..." maps to src/ (tsconfig paths)
 * - extensionless relative imports resolve to .ts / .tsx / index.ts
 * - "server-only" resolves to an empty module (Next handles it in builds)
 * - "next/<name>" resolves to next/<name>.js: Next has no exports map, so Node
 *   ESM needs the extension that bundlers add for it (e.g. next/dynamic)
 *
 * .tsx modules contain JSX, which type stripping cannot parse, so they are
 * compiled with the project's own TypeScript compiler. It is loaded the first
 * time a .tsx file is imported, so test files that never import one pay nothing.
 */
import { existsSync, readFileSync, statSync } from "node:fs";
import { createRequire, registerHooks } from "node:module";
import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from "node:url";

const SRC_DIR = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../src/", import.meta.url));
const EMPTY_MODULE = "data:text/javascript,export {};";
const nodeRequire = createRequire(import.meta.url);
let typescript;

function compileTsx(url) {
  typescript ??= nodeRequire("typescript");
  const fileName = fileURLToPath(url);
  const { outputText } = typescript.transpileModule(readFileSync(fileName, "utf8"), {
    fileName,
    compilerOptions: {
      jsx: typescript.JsxEmit.ReactJSX,
      module: typescript.ModuleKind.ESNext,
      target: typescript.ScriptTarget.ES2022,
    },
  });
  return outputText;
}

function resolveFile(basePath) {
  for (const candidate of [basePath, `${basePath}.ts`, `${basePath}.tsx`, `${basePath}/index.ts`]) {
    if (existsSync(candidate) && statSync(candidate).isFile()) return candidate;
  }
  return null;
}

registerHooks({
  resolve(specifier, context, nextResolve) {
    i<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="5839" />ould not be read, and a safe copy of it could not be made, so nothing was changed. Download your data from the journal first.";
    default:
      return "This could not be saved. Please try again.";
  }
}

function commit(change: (record: LearningRecord) => LearningRecord): ActionResult {
  const current = read();
  if (current.status !== "ready") return { ok: false, message: failure({ ok: false, reason: "unavailable" }) };
  let next: LearningRecord;
  try {
    next = change(current.record);
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof ReflectionError || error instanceof SolveError) return { ok: false, message: error.message };
    throw error;
  }
  if (next === current.record) return { ok: true };
  const result = getStore().write(next);
  for (const listener of listeners) listener();
  return result.ok ? { ok: true } : { ok: false, message: failure(result) };
}

export const learningActions: LearningActions = {
  recordSolve: (challengeId) => commit((record) => recordObservedSolve(record, challengeId, new Date())),
  saveReflection: (challengeId, reflection) => commit((record) => saveReflection(record, challengeId, reflection, new Date())),
  deleteReflection: (challengeId) => commit((record) => deleteReflection(record, challengeId)),
  clearReflections: () => commit((record) => clearReflections(record)),
  recordRecall: (challengeId, response) => commit((record) => recordRecall(record, challengeId, response, new Date())),
  recordPracticeOpened: (from, to) => commit((record) => recordPracticeOpened(record, from, to, new Date())),
  exportData: () => getStore().exportData(new Date()),
};

/** The learning record and the actions that change it. Loading until the browser has read storage. */
export function useLearning(): { state: LearningState; actions: LearningActions } {
  const state = useSyncExternalStore<LearningState>(subscribe, read, () => LOADING);
  return { state, actions: learningActions };
}

export interface ReflectionEntry {
  challengeId: string;
  reflection: Reflection;

/** A related challenge the learner opened from an Apply suggestion. */

}

export interface LearningRecord {

  reflections: Record<string, ReflectionEntry>;

}

export const EMPTY_RECORD: LearningRecord = Object.freeze({
  version: LEARNING_VERSION,

}) as unknown as LearningRecord;

export const MAX_STAGE = 2;
const CHALLENGE_ID = /^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$/;

export const isRecord = (value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> =>
  typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
export const isTime = (value: unknown): value is string => typeof value === "string" && !Number.isNaN(Date.parse(value));
export const isLocalDay = (value: unknown): value is LocalDay => typeof value === "string" && LOCAL_DAY.test(value);
export const isChallengeId = (value: unknown): value is string => typeof value === "string" && CHALLENGE_ID.test(value);
const isStage = (value: unknown): value is number => typeof value === "number" && Number.isInteger(value) && value >= 0 && value <= MAX_STAGE;

/** Trims a learner's text and cuts it to the limit. */
export function cleanText(value: unknown, max: number): string {
  return typeof value === "string" ? value.replace(/\r\n?/g, "\n").trim().slice(0, max) : "";
}

import { SessionChallengeOverview } from "@/components/challenge/SessionChallengeOverview";
import { AppHeader } from "@/components/layout/AppHeader";
import { SkipLinks } from "@/components/layout/SkipLinks";
import { WorkspaceClient } from "@/components/workspace/WorkspaceClient";
import { challengePath, ROUTES } from "@/lib/challenge-routes";
import type { WorkspaceScenario } from "@/types/workspace";
import { ChallengeJourney, type ChallengeLink } from "./ChallengeJourney";

interface ChallengeWorkspacePageProps {
  /** Learner-visible scenario data only. Answer keys never pass through here. */
  scenario: WorkspaceScenario;
  /** The next published challenge, offered once this one is solved; null for the last. */
  next?: { id: string; number: string; title: string } | null;
}

/** The full workspace page for one challenge: header, overview and workspace. */
export function ChallengeWorkspacePage({ scenario, next = null }: ChallengeWorkspacePageProps) {
  const nextLink: ChallengeLink | null = next ? { href: challengePath(next.id), number: next.number, title: next.title } : null;
  return (
    <>
      <SkipLinks />
      <AppHeader current="workspace" />
      <main className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-[1600px] flex-1 flex-col gap-3 px-4 py-4 sm:px-6">
        <ChallengeJourney challenge={scenario.challenge} libraryHref={ROUTES.library} next={nextLink}>
          <SessionChallengeOverview challenge={scenario.challenge} />
          <WorkspaceClient
            challengeId={scenario.challenge.id}
            rootCauseOptions={scenario.rootCauseOptions}
            files={scenario.files}
            entryFilePath={scenario.entryFilePath}
            incidentDate={scenario.incidentDate}
            logs={scenario.logs}
            network={scenario.network}
          />
        </ChallengeJourney>
      </main>
    </>
  );
}

# Learner session and attempt foundation

| | |
|---|---|
| **Status** | **Implemented**, server side only, **not wired into any endpoint or page**. Wiring it needs Syeda's approval (section 11). |
| **Branch** | `feature/learner-session-foundation`, built on `feature/server-attested-session-foundation` (PR #2) |
| **Code** | `src/server/session/*`, and the challenge loader and registry in `src/server/challenges/*` (section 8) |
| **Tests** | `tests/session/*`, `tests/challenges/*` |
| **Storage** | In-memory development and test adapter only. **Data is lost on every process restart. This is not durable persistence.** |

This document says what exists. Anything labelled **Deferred** is not built, and nothing here is a commitment to build it.

## 1. What this is

A storage-independent backend foundation for three things, one inside the next:

| Concept | What it is | Identified by | Lives as long as |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Learner** | One anonymous identity for the whole product, across every challenge | A random 256-bit token in the `buglab_learner` cookie. The server stores only its SHA-256 hash. | Idle expiry (sliding) and absolute expiry (fixed), from `expiry.ts` |
| **Challenge session** | One learner's run at one challenge. At most one per learner and challenge. | No token. It is reached only through its learner. | Its learner |
| **Dia<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="3047" />.

export function AppHeader({ current }: AppHeaderProps) {
  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-10 border-b border-border bg-nav/90 shadow-nav backdrop-blur">
      <div className="mx-auto flex h-14 max-w-[1600px] items-center gap-3 px-4 sm:px-6 md:gap-8">
        <Link
          href={ROUTES.home}
          className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2.5 rounded-md focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-accent"
        >
          <span className="flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-md border border-accent/30 bg-accent/10 text-accent">
            <BugIcon width={18} height={18} />
          </span>
          <span className="flex flex-col leading-none">
            <span className="text-sm font-semibold tracking-tight text-foreground">BugLab</span>
            <span className="mt-1 hidden text-xs text-subtle lg:block">Debugging investigations</span>
          </span>
        </Link>

        <nav aria-label="Primary" className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 justify-end md:justify-start">
          <ul className="hidden items-center gap-1 text-sm md:flex">
            {navItems.map((item) => (
              <li key={item.label}>
                <NavEntry item={item} current={current} />
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
          <MobileMenu items={navItems} current={current} />
        </nav>

        <ThemeToggle />
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

function NavEntry({ item, current }: { item: NavItem; current: AppSection }) {
  const isCurrent = item.section === current;

  if (isCurrent && item.textWhenCurrent) {
    return (
      <span aria-current="page" className={navItemClass(true)}>
        {item.label}
      </span>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Link href={item.href} aria-current={isCurrent ? "page" : undefined} className={navItemClass(isCurrent)}>
      {item.label}
    </Link>
  );
}

import type { DiagnosisResponse } from "@/types/diagnosis";
import type { InvestigationStatus } from "@/types/workspace";
import { diagnosisResponseToEvent, isRejectedDiagnosis } from "../adapters/diagnosis";
import type { SessionConfig, SessionState } from "../types";
import { createSession, sessionReducer } from "./reducer";
import { deriveInvestigationStatus } from "./status";

/**
 * What the learner has done in one challenge: the engine session plus the one
 * signal the session deliberately cannot hold. Plain data with pure transitions,
 * so the React provider is only a thin owner of it.
 */
export interface LearnerProgress {
  session: SessionState;
  /**
   * True once the server has rejected a diagnosis (`incorrect`). Latched: nothing
   * clears it, so revising the diagnosis form never erases it.
   */
  diagnosisSubmitted: boolean;
}

export function createProgress(config: SessionConfig): LearnerProgress {
  return { session: createSession(config), diagnosisSubmitted: false };
}

/**
 * Applies a server diagnosis response, unchanged, to the progress. Only the
 * adapter's event (a `correct` response) touches the session; only `incorrect`
 * latches `diagnosisSubmitted`. Any other response returns the same object.
 */
export function applyDiagnosisResponse(
  progress: LearnerProgress,
  response: DiagnosisResponse,
): LearnerProgress {
  const event = diagnosisResponseToEvent(response);
  const session = event ? sessionReducer(progress.session, event) : progress.session;
  const diagnosisSubmitted = progress.diagnosisSubmitted || isRejectedDiagnosis(response);

  return session === progress.session && diagnosisSubmitted === progress.diagnosisSubmitted
    ? progress
    : { session, diagnosisSubmitted };
}

export function progressStatus(
  progress: LearnerProgres<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="2939" />iedState = Object.freeze({ status: "not-connected" });

/*
 * Lets Node's built-in test runner load the app's TypeScript modules
 * (Node 24 strips types natively). Module resolution is customised:
 * - "@/..." maps to src/ (tsconfig paths)
 * - extensionless relative imports resolve to .ts / .tsx / index.ts
 * - "server-only" resolves to an empty module (Next handles it in builds)
 * - "next/<name>" resolves to next/<name>.js: Next has no exports map, so Node
 *   ESM needs the extension that bundlers add for it (e.g. next/dynamic)
 *
 * .tsx modules contain JSX, which type stripping cannot parse, so they are
 * compiled with the project's own TypeScript compiler. It is loaded the first
 * time a .tsx file is imported, so test files that never import one pay nothing.
 */
import { existsSync, readFileSync, statSync } from "node:fs";
import { createRequire, registerHooks } from "node:module";
import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from "node:url";

const SRC_DIR = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../src/", import.meta.url));
const EMPTY_MODULE = "data:text/javascript,export {};";
const nodeRequire = createRequire(import.meta.url);
let typescript;

function compileTsx(url) {
  typescript ??= nodeRequire("typescript");
  const fileName = fileURLToPath(url);
  const { outputText } = typescript.transpileModule(readFileSync(fileName, "utf8"), {
    fileName,
    compilerOptions: {
      jsx: typescript.JsxEmit.ReactJSX,
      module: typescript.ModuleKind.ESNext,
      target: typescript.ScriptTarget.ES2022,
    },
  });
  return outputText;
}

function resolveFile(basePath) {
  for (const candidate of [basePath, `${basePath}.ts`, `${basePath}.tsx`, `${basePath}/index.ts`]) {
    if (existsSync(candidate) && statSync(candidate).isFile()) return candidate;
  }

function parseAttempts(raw: unknown): RecallAttempt[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(raw)) return [];
  return raw
    .filter(
      (attempt): attempt is RecallAttempt =>
        isRecord(attempt) && isTime(attempt.at) && isLocalDay(attempt.day) && (attempt.response === "got-it" || attempt.response === "need-practice"),
    )
    .map(({ at, day, response }) => ({ at, day, response }))
    .slice(-LIMITS.attempts);
}

function parseReflection(id: string, raw: unknown): ReflectionEntry | null {
  if (!isChallengeId(id) || !isRecord(raw) || raw.challengeId !== id) return null;
  if (!isRecord(raw.reflection) || !isRecord(raw.recall)) return null;
  if (!isTime(raw.reflectedAt) || !isTime(raw.updatedAt)) return null;
  const recall = raw.recall;
  if (!isStage(recall.stage) || !isStage(recall.stageBefore) || !isLocalDay(recall.dueOn)) return null;
  if (recall.lastReviewedOn !== null && !isLocalDay(recall.lastReviewedOn)) return null;
  const reflection = cleanReflection(raw.reflection as Partial<Reflection>);
  if (!reflection.takeaway) return null;
  return {
    challengeId: id,
    reflection,
    reflectedAt: raw.reflectedAt,
    updatedAt: raw.updatedAt,
    recall: {
      stage: recall.stage,
      dueOn: recall.dueOn,
      lastReviewedOn: recall.lastReviewedOn,
      stageBefore: recall.stageBefore,
      attempts: parseAttempts(recall.attempts),
    },
  };
}

import { THEME_INIT_SCRIPT } from "@/lib/theme";
import "./globals.css";

const geistSans = Geist({
  variable: "--font-geist-sans",
  subsets: ["latin"],
});

const geistMono = Geist_Mono({
  variable: "--font-geist-mono",
  subsets: ["latin"],
});

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "BugLab Â· Debugging Workspace",
  description:
    "Learn software engineering through realistic debugging investigations.",
};

export default function RootLayout({ children }: LayoutProps<"/">) {
  return (
    // The <head> script sets data-theme on <html> before hydration, so React
    // must not treat that attribute as a mismatch (this <html> element only).
    <html
      lang="en"
      className={`${geistSans.variable} ${geistMono.variable} h-full antialiased`}
      suppressHydrationWarning
    >
      <head>
        {/* Applies the saved or system theme before the first paint: no flash of the wrong theme. */}
        <script dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: THEME_INIT_SCRIPT }} />
      </head>
      <body className="min-h-full flex flex-col bg-background text-foreground">{children}</body>
    </html>
  );
}

      assert.doesNotMatch(code, /@\/server\/session|buglab_learner|LearnerStore|MemoryStore/, rel(path));

describe("no unprotected admin or account routes", () => {
  const appFiles = walk(join(ROOT, "app")).map(rel);

    assert.deepEqual(exposed, []);

  });

    try {

  test("no file under src/ contains a pinned digest", () => {
    const digests = Object.values(PINS).map((pin) => pin.evaluationDigest);
    for (const path of sourceFiles(join(ROOT, "src"))) {
      const text = readFileSync(path, "utf8");
      for (const digest of digests) assert.ok(!text.includes(digest), path);

  test("the content version does not contain any part of the digest", () => {
    const definitions = definitionsOf(defaultChallengeSources);
    const registry = createChallengeRegistry(definitions);
    for (const definition of definitions) {
      const version = registry.contentVersion(idOf(definition))!;
      const digest = digestOf(definition);
      assert.ok(!version.includes(digest.slice(0, 12)), "the version must not be derived from the private configuration");
    }
  });

  // Reported as skipped, not passed, when there is no build to look at: run `npm run build` first to include it.
  test(
    "no built client output contains a pinned digest",
    { skip: hasBuildOutput() ? false : "no .next/static: run the production build first to include this scan" },
    () => {
      const digests = Object.values(PINS).map((pin) => pin.evaluationDigest);
      let scanned = 0;
      for (const path of sourceFiles(BUILD_OUTPUT)) {
        if (!/\.(js|css|html|json|txt)$/.test(path)) continue;
        scanned += 1;
        const text = readFileSync(path, "utf8");
        for (const digest of digests) assert.ok(!text.includes(digest), path);
      }
      assert.ok(scanned > 0, "the build output was empty, so the scan proved nothing");
    },
  );
});

/*
 * Rejects challenge URLs that are not exactly a catalog challenge's path, before
 * Next.js reads or writes its route cache. That cache stores each challenge URL
 * as a file named after the URL, so on a case-insensitive file system (Windows,
 * default macOS) a cached 404 for /challenges/CHALLENGE-001 overwrites the page
 * for /challenges/challenge-001. Rejecting here also stops unknown IDs from
 * adding a cache entry each. The page keeps its own notFound() check.
 */
import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from "next/server";
import { listChallengeSummaries } from "@/lib/challenge-catalog";
import { challengePath } from "@/lib/challenge-routes";

/** Every playable challenge path, compared as requested: no decoding, exact case. */
const CHALLENGE_PATHS: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(listChallengeSummaries().map(({ id }) => challengePath(id)));

export function proxy(request: NextRequest) {
  if (CHALLENGE_PATHS.has(request.nextUrl.pathname)) return NextResponse.next();
  return new NextResponse("Challenge not found.", {
    status: 404,
    headers: { "content-type": "text/plain; charset=utf-8", "cache-control": "no-store" },
  });
}

export const config = {
  matcher: "/challenges/:challengeId",
};

  test("published metadata reveals nothing about the answer", () => {
    const published = JSON.stringify(listPublishedChallenges());
    for (const field of ["correctRootCauseId", "resolutionSummary", "keyObservations", "keyEvidence", "rootCauseOptions", "files"]) {
      assert.ok(!published.includes(field), field);
    }
    assert.ok(!published.includes(provisionalAnswerKey.resolutionSummary));
    const correct = challenge001.rootCauseOptions.find((option) => option.id === provisionalAnswerKey.correctRootCauseId)!;
    assert.ok(!published.includes(correct.label), "the correct option's label");
    assert.ok(!published.includes(correct.id), "the correct option's id");
  });
});

describe("who may read the catalog", () => {
  test("only server code imports the catalog; no client component can bundle scenarios", async () => {
    const { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } = await import("node:fs");
    const { join } = await import("node:path");
    const { fileURLToPath } = await import("node:url");
    const root = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../src/", import.meta.url));
    const walk = (dir: string): string[] =>
      readdirSync(dir).flatMap((name) => {
        const path = join(dir, name);
        return statSync(path).isDirectory() ? walk(path) : /\.tsx?$/.test(name) ? [path] : [];
      });
    const users = walk(root)
      .filter((path) => /@\/lib\/challenge-catalog|@\/data\/catalog["']/.test(readFileSync(path, "utf8")))
      .map((path) => path.slice(root.length).replaceAll("\\", "/"))
      .sort();
    assert.deepEqual(users, [
      "app/challenges/[challengeId]/page.tsx",
      "app/challenges/page.tsx",
      "app/journal/page.tsx",
      "app/progress/page.tsx",
      "lib/challenge-catalog.ts",
      "proxy.ts",
      "server/admin/challenges.ts",
      "server/diagnosis/published.ts",
    ]);
    for (const path of users) assert.doesNotMatch(readFileSync(join(root, path), "utf8"), /["']use client["']/, path);
  });
});

The current catalog has 30 published cases.\n\nKnown verified completions are excluded regardless of content version and local\nopt-out. Local observed solves independently suppress new work when enabled;\nthey never create verified completions. Missing history does not prove a case\nis unsolved elsewhere, and the UI says so.\n\nInitial bounded integer weights:\n\n| Factor | Maximum | Basis |\n|---|---:|---|\n| Difficulty fit | 30 | Preferred/current tier 30; gentler practice 15; optional next tier 5 |\n| Goal match | 20 | Explicit selected category |\n| Practice request | 20 | Category of an actual local `need-practice` recall |\n| Coverage opportunity | 15 | Floor of `15 * (available - represented) / available` |\n| Continuity | 10 | Shared public category/topic with an actual recent completion/observation |\n| Time fit | 5 | Catalog estimate within the selected budget |\n\nThe total is a ranking value, not a probability or proficiency score. Missing\nfactors contribute zero; the total is not renormalized. Coverage uses complete\nverified history when available, otherwise a ready browser record. Partial data\nnever becomes proof that a category was not practiced. Recency uses verified\nfacts first, then local observations; future-dated observations do not establish\nrecency. No attempt count, success rate, time-to-solve or skill mastery is invented.\n\nFocus and time are preferences, not hard filters. Difficulty is a preferred tier\nand upper bound, with gentler cases allowed. Stretch explicitly permits one\nhigher tier. Static estimates are never presented as actual completion times.\n\nTies use score descending, numeric challenge number, then ASCII ID. Duplicate\ncompletion versions have a stable tie-break; conflicting difficulty evidence is\nignored rather than allowing input order to decide.\n\n## Paths, coverage and difficulty\n\nA cold start prefers beginner investigations; featured metadata cannot override\nfit. Greedy path selection adds a bounded diversity penalty of 10 per already\nselected case in a category (maximum 20), unless a focus is explicit. A reason\nexplains when a new area adds variety. Path selection never simulates progress\nor completion.\n\nThere are at most **five distinct new-work challenge IDs overall**. `next` is the\nfirst path item; up to three practice highlights reference other steps already\nin that same path, not three extra challenges. Recall is a separate review action.\nFewer cases are returned if the eligible pool is exhausted.\n\nThe six catalog categories are practice-coverage areas, not measured skills.\nTags explain relatedness. No weakness/mastery percentages are inferred.\n\nDefault difficulty is beginner. An explicit preference wins. Current completion DTOs do not\nprovide those historical facts, so production does not synthesize them from\ncurrent catalog labels. Repeated solves, local flags, missing attempts, and\nself-reported recall do not trigger a proficiency assessment or automatic demotion.\n\n## Recall\n\nThe read-only browser adapter calls the existing `dueReflections` selector, which\nowns the established 1/3/7 local-calendar-day schedule. The engine validates dates\nagain and orders due/overdue items by due date then catalog order. Leap days and\nDST boundaries use existing calendar arithmetic. Upcoming or unknown/withdrawn\ncases are not exposed. Verified completion alone creates no reflection or recall.\nCompleted cases may appear as recall, never as new work.\n\nRecall cards link to `/journal#recall`; this feature neither copies the Journal's\nrecall UI nor changes its scheduling. A Journal consumer can still show the\nlearner's own withdrawn-case history; it is not recommended here.\n\n## UI and states\n\nThe dedicated page includes a next investigation, numbered path, practice\nhighlights, existing due recalls, preference controls and typed factual reasons.\nIt uses existing surfaces, typography, theme tokens, difficulty badges, icons,\nfooter and theme toggle. Changing preferences\naborts obsolete requests and hides stale results; a 15-second timeout is retryable.\nBlocked storage retains catalog suggestions. Partial/unavailable/expired history\nis disclosed without fabricated counts. Empty ranges can be adjusted or opened\nin the library. API errors never display raw server text. Reduced-motion loading\nstates are supported. Existing browser records remain byte-for-byte unchanged.\n\n## Integration gates\n\nBefore authenticated personalization is enabled, Claude and Habin must agree:\n\n1. The mounted, authorized verified-completion contract and coverage semantics.\n2. A durable identity/persistence source; no development MemoryStore activation.\n3. Explicit consent/ownership rules for associating browser-local observations.\n4. Identity-change invalidation: abort old requests, clear displayed plans, use\n   a subject-generation key, and never retain another subject's previous history.\n5. How same-origin credentials, origin policy and cache controls apply. The\n   initial client deliberately omits credentials and must be changed explicitly.\n6. Version-matched historical difficulty evidence, if progression is enabled.\n\nImplement `VerifiedHistoryProvider` per request only after those gates. Never\nresolve identity from a body field or arbitrary caller-supplied header. The\ninjected test provider's `x-test-subject` header is a fixture, not an identity\nimplementation. Do not add a module-global learner or personalized result cache.\nAll API responses are private/no-store, including errors. Private reflections,\nfeedback and completion submissions remain outside this contract.\n\nA future Progress widget can consume `next`, `recalls`, `target`, `evidence` and\n`notices` from this same plan. It must preserve provenance and leave Journal and\nProgress selectors as their owners' responsibility. `RecommendationEngine` is\nan explicit strategy interface; a future model strategy would require a version\nreview and the same hard eligibility/privacy constraints. No model service is\nintroduced in V1.\n\n## Verification and worktree setup\n\n```powershell\nnpm run lint\nnpm run typecheck\nnpm run build\nnpm test\nnpm run start -- --port 3107\nnode scripts/verify-recommendations-browser.mjs\n```\n\nThe browser script uses installed Microsoft Edge, an isolated profile under\n`.next/`, and localhost only. It installs nothing, changes no real browser data,\nand closes its own browser. Screenshots and reports are ignored build artifacts.\nIt covers preferences, recall, local solve suppression, opt-out, private-writing\nexclusion, unchanged storage, both themes, mobile overflow, keyboard navigation,\ncontrast, blocked requests/retry and unavailable storage.\n\nThe initial worktree dependency junction pointed outside Turbopack's inferred\nroot and caused `next/package.json` resolution to fail. This is a recommendation\nendpoint, not a mounted learner API. There is no separate service or paid model.\n\n## GET: catalog starting points\n\n```text\nGET /api/recommendations/v1\nGET /api/recommendations/v1?category=frontend&difficulty=beginner&minutes=20&stretch=0&today=2026-11-06\n```\n\nAll query fields are optional. Unknown/repeated fields are rejected. Category\nmust be one of the six public category IDs; difficulty is beginner/intermediate/\nadvanced; minutes is an integer 5–180; stretch is exactly `0` or `1`; today is a\nreal `YYYY-MM-DD` calendar day. An omitted day does not invent a learner timezone.\nGET supplies no browser activity and never creates a learner.\n\n## POST: explicitly browser-local guidance\n\n`Content-Type: application/json` (optional charset). All fields below are\nrequired; null means no preference. Unknown properties at every input level\nare rejected. Do not submit a whole LearningRecord or verified-completion view.\n\n```json\n{\n  \"version\": 1,\n  \"today\": \"2026-11-06\",\n  \"preferences\": {\n    \"category\": \"frontend\",\n    \"difficulty\": \"beginner\",\n    \"minutes\": 20,\n    \"allowStretch\": false,\n    \"useLocalSignals\": true\n  },\n  \"local\": {\n    \"status\": \"ready\",\n    \"solves\": [],\n    \"recalls\": [],\n    \"practiceRequests\": []\n  }\n}\n```\n\n- `today`: a real local calendar day, or null (no due-recall calculation).\n- `preferences.category`: a public category ID or null; a soft focus.\n- `preferences.difficulty`: supported tier or null; preferred tier/upper bound.\n- `preferences.minutes`: integer 5–180 or null; soft catalog-estimate budget.\n- `allowStretch`, `useLocalSignals`: strict booleans, no coercion.\n- `local.status`: `ready`, `partial`, or `unavailable`.\n- `solves`: at most 300 `{challengeId,lastSolvedAt}` local observations; UTC\n  ISO instants, not server completion timestamps. Source remains browser-local.\n- `recalls`: at most 100 `{challengeId,dueOn}` existing browser schedule entries;\n  dates are validated and future entries are not due.\n- `practiceRequests`: at most 100 challenge IDs whose local recalls requested\n  practice. These are self-reports, not failed diagnosis attempts.\n- IDs: lowercase hyphenated strings, maximum 100 characters. Unknown IDs are\n  ignored by eligibility; the browser adapter additionally suppresses all IDs\n  outside the published/playable allow-list before transmission.\n- `unavailable` local data must contain empty lists. Partial data never establishes\n  missing-category coverage. Disabling local signals ignores all local evidence.\n\nBody size is bounded to 32,768 UTF-8 bytes, including streamed bodies without a\nContent-Length header. Body reading has a five-second deadline. Invalid JSON,\nUTF-8, dates, shapes, types, versions, counts, and properties are rejected.\n\nRequests containing `verified`, `learnerId`, `accountId`, catalog content, private\nreflection fields or credential fields are invalid. Merely labelling a browser\nobservation verified cannot bypass this boundary.\n\n## Response: RecommendationPlan\n\nSuccess is 200, including a useful cold start or a plan with unavailable sources.\nThe TypeScript contract is `src/lib/recommendations/types.ts`:\n\n- `version: 1`, `strategy: deterministic-v1`.\n- `mode`: cold-start, personalized, or limited-data. Not a confidence score.\n- `target`: difficulty and basis (default/preference/verified-exposure).\n- `evidence`: separate verified-source and local-source statuses.\n- `availableCount`, `eligibleCount`: public availability and eligible new work.\n- `next`: first path item, or null.\n- `path`: zero to five distinct challenge recommendations.\n- `practice`: zero to three highlights of other steps already in that path.\n  Across next/path/practice there are at most five distinct new-work IDs.\n- `recalls`: existing due/overdue browser recalls, ordered oldest first; separate\n  from new work. Their challenge may be completed, but is never new-work eligible.\n- `notices`: typed history/storage limitations. Missing data is not empty history.\n\nChallenge recommendations contain a public metadata allow-list, bounded scoring\nfactors and typed factual reasons. Reasons have codes/bases and numeric/date/\ncategory parameters; no arbitrary server prose, user writing or grading fields.\nThe UI renders owned templates from `reasons.ts`. Scores are internal ordering\nvalues, not measured skill, success rates or probabilities.\n\nUnknown/unpublished IDs, account identity, raw completion objects, private\nreflections, resolution text, feedback, submissions and hidden grading data are\nnever serialized in a successful recommendation response.\n\n## Errors and privacy\n\n```json\n{\"version\":1,\"error\":{\"code\":\"invalid-request\",\"message\":\"The recommendation request is invalid.\"}}\n```\n\n| Status | Code / meaning |\n|---|---|\n| 400 | invalid-request: unsupported fields, invalid query/body/version |\n| 403 | refused: cross-site Fetch Metadata or mismatched Origin |\n| 405 | unsupported HTTP method (Next handles methods not exported by the route) |\n| 408 | request-timeout: body read deadline |\n| 413 | payload-too-large: declared or actual byte limit |\n| 415 | unsupported-media-type |\n| 503 | recommendations-unavailable: catalog/engine failure |\n\nError bodies never echo request values, exception messages, provider failures,\ncredentials or reflection writing. Provider failure normally returns a 200\nlimited-data/cold-start plan with verified status unavailable, not a fabricated\nready/empty result. No request bodies are logged by feature code.\n\nGET/POST responses and feature errors use `Cache-Control: private, no-store,\nmax-age=0`, `Pragma: no-cache`, `Vary: Cookie, Authorization, Origin`, and nosniff.\nNo CORS access is granted. The initial browser client uses `credentials: omit`\nand `cache: no-store`. No per-learner or personalized module-level cache exists.\n\n## Authenticated integration boundary\n\n`createRecommendationHandler` accepts an internal `VerifiedHistoryProvider`.\nThe mounted route deliberately uses its disconnected default. The provider must\nresolve the authorized subject through Claude/Habin's agreed identity layer;\nbody IDs or arbitrary headers are not identity. Results are sanitized and used\nonly for the current request. `ready` explicitly declares complete/partial\ncoverage and carries only authoritative completion DTOs. Other states remain\nnot-connected/no-learner/expired/unavailable.\n\nOptional future difficulty evidence must name the same completed challenge and\ncontent version and its actual historical difficulty. It cannot be inferred\nfrom today's catalog or from local solve counts. Conflicting evidence is ignored.\n\nBefore activation, agree authorization, persistence, local-data consent,\nidentity-change invalidation, credential policy and same-origin protections.\nDo not mount shared learner routes, link accounts, read private reflections,\nactivate production services or add a database as part of this feature.\n'@\n$utf8 = New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)\nforeach ($relative in $files.Keys) { $target = Join-Path (Get-Location) $relative; [System.IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory([System.IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($target)) | Out-Null; [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($target, $files[$relative], $utf8) }\nWrite-Output 'Documented API v1, source provenance, ownership, integration gates, scoring, verification and exact feature manifest.'"
